B.1.1.
Type preservation

Lemma B.1 (The interpretation of a zeroed Graded Base context gives a Linear Base context
that is graded at zero).

For all graded-base contexts
\Delta
, the predicate
\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})
holds.

Proof.

By induction on the structure of
\Delta
.

•

(empty context)
For
\emptyset
, we prove the goal
\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\emptyset{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})
syntactically:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}\textit{(defn. graded)}&&\mathrm{graded}(\emptyset,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\\
\textit{(defn. interepretation)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\emptyset{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\\
\textit{(defn. multiplication)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\emptyset{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\end{array}

•

(graded context extension)
For
\Delta,\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}
,

By induction, we have that
\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})
, from which we conclude
with the following steps:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}\textit{(i.h.)}&&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\\
\textit{(defn. graded)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{0}}},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\\
\textit{(defn. interpretation)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}({\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta),\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0}}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\\
\textit{(defn. multiplication)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}(\Delta,\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}){\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\end{array}

∎

Lemma B.2 (Interpretation of a graded context is graded in Linear Base).

For all contexts
\Delta
,
\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket})
.

Proof.

By induction over the structure of
\Delta
.

•

(empty context)

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}\textit{(defn. graded)}&&\mathrm{graded}(\emptyset)\\
\textit{(defn. interpretation)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\emptyset{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket})\end{array}

•

(graded context extension)

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}\textit{(i.h.)}&&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket})\\
\textit{(defn. graded)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}})\\
\textit{(defn. interpretation)}&\implies&\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta,\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket})\end{array}

∎

Proof.

By induction on the typing derivations of Graded Base.

•

(var)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{var${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

Therefore we construct the goal typing:

\frac{\frac{}{\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={var${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}{\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={der${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}

•

(abs)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\Delta,\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{B}\\
\end{array}}{\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{abs${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise we have
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Then we construct, with
y\#t
:

\frac{\frac{\frac{}{\mathit{y}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{y}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{Right={var${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{ih}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{y}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={let${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\multimap{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={abs${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}

which satisfies the goal.

•

(app)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{B}&\;\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{app${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

By induction we have:
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\multimap{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

and
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Then we construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\multimap{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\quad\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={pr${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={app${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}

Since translation of contexts is a homomorphism then:

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

•

(weak)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}\\
\end{array}}{\Delta,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{weak${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premises we have
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Therefore we construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\quad\mathrm{graded}({\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\Delta^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={weak${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}

where the second premise is provided by Lemma
B.1
.

•

(approx)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Delta,\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{B}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}&\;\mathit{r}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\sqsubseteq}\,\mathit{s}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Delta,\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{s}}}\mathit{B}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{approx${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise we have

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{s}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Therefore we can construct:

\frac{\begin{array}[]{cc}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{s}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\;\mathit{s}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\sqsubseteq}\,\mathit{r}\end{array}}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{s}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={approx${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}

∎

B.1.2.
Operational correspondence

Lemma B.3 (Interpretation preserves substitution).

For all Graded Base terms
\mathit{t},\mathit{t^{\prime}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Proof.

By induction on the receiving term
\mathit{t^{\prime}}
:

•

(var)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\mathit{y}
:

–

\mathit{x}\equiv\mathit{y}
then we refine the goal:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{x}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\
\textit{(reflexivity)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}

–

\mathit{x}\not\equiv\mathit{y}
then we refine the goal:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathit{y}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathit{y}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{y}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathit{y}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\mathit{y}\\
\textit{(reflexivity)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}

∎

The operational correspondence now follows via the following proof:

Proof.

For convenience, we expand the statement to
\mathit{t}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t^{\prime}}

then
\exists t^{\prime\prime}.{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}

\,\wedge\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}
.

By induction on reductions:

•

(beta)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\beta_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket})\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}

where
y\#t
.
Then we construct the following reduction sequence in Linear Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket})\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{L}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{L}}${}}

And since
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

by Lemma
B.3

then the goal is satisfied.

•

(congAppL)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}

By induction on
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, we have

\exists\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\wedge\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
. Therefore, we construct
a chain of reductions in Linear Base:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{i}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{i}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}${}}\;\;\cdots\;\;\frac{\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{m}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}{\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{m}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}${}}

where
m
is the length of the reduction sequence from
the induction.

satisfying the goal, since
\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

and therefore

\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}
.

∎

B.1.3.
Equation preservation

Proof.

By induction on the definition of the equational theory.

•

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv_{\textsc{g}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\beta}$}{}}}

Follows by Theorem
2.3.1
(preservation of operational
semantics), since this includes the
\beta
case as a reduction.

•

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{x}\,\#\,\mathit{t}\\
\end{array}}{\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t}\,\mathit{x}\equiv_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\eta}$}{}}}

Preservation then follows by:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t}\,\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\\
\textit{\lx@text@lbrace defn. translation\lx@text@rbrace}&=\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\\
\textit{\lx@text@lbrace$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\mathsf{cong}\lambda}${}+$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\mathsf{let}\gamma_{1}}$\lx@text@rbrace}&\equiv_{L}\lambda\mathit{y}.{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,(\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]})\\
\textit{\lx@text@lbrace$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\mathsf{cong}\lambda}${}+$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\Box\beta}$\lx@text@rbrace}&\equiv_{L}\lambda\mathit{y}.{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,\mathit{y}\\
\textit{\lx@text@lbrace$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\eta}$\lx@text@rbrace}&\equiv_{L}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\end{array}

•

The remaining rules are congruences and all follow by induction with
the translation.

∎

B.2.
Proof of Soundness for Graded Base to Linear Base

2.3.2

B.2.1
,
operational correspondence in Appendix
B.2.2
,
and equation preservation in Appendix
B.2.3
.

B.2.1.
Type preservation

Lemma B.4 (CPS form).

For all types
\mathit{A}
then

\exists\mathit{B}.\ {\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}=\mathit{B}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K}
.

Proof.

By cases on the CPS translation
∎

Proof.

By induction on the Linear Base typing:

•

(var)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{var${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By lemma
B.4
, let
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}=\mathit{B}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K}

for some
B
.

Therefore we construct the goal typing:

\frac{\frac{\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}(\mathit{B}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K})\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{x}:(\mathit{B}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K})\hskip 8.19447pt\mathit{k}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathit{B}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{k}:\mathit{B}}{\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}(\mathit{B}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K}),\mathit{k}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathit{B}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{x}\,\mathit{k}:\mathrm{K}}\quad\textsc{Right={app${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}{\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}(\mathit{B}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K})\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{x}\,\mathit{k}:(\mathit{B}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K})}\quad\textsc{Right={abs${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

(app)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}&\;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{app${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction we have that:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:(({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K})\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

and by lemma
B.4
,
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}=\mathit{C}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathrm{K}
for some

.

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{B}\\

and by lemma
B.4

B.2.2.
Operational correspondence

Lemma B.5 (Interpretation preserves substitution).

For all Linear Base terms
\mathit{t},\mathit{t^{\prime}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Proof.

By induction on the receiving term
\mathit{t^{\prime}}
:

•

(var)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\mathit{y}
:

–

\mathit{x}\equiv\mathit{y}
then we refine the goal:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{x}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{x}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{x}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{x}\,\mathit{k}\\
\textit{($\equiv_{\eta}[rhs]$)}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{x}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(reflexivity)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}

–

\mathit{x}\not\equiv\mathit{y}
then we refine the goal:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{y}\,\mathit{k}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{y}\,\mathit{k}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{y}\,\mathit{k}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{y}\,\mathit{k}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{y}\,\mathit{k}\\
\textit{(reflexivity)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}

(abs)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
(with
\mathit{k},\mathit{y}\,\#\,\mathit{t}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}](\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\lambda\mathit{y}.[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\

\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\lambda\mathit{y}.[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{k}\,(\lambda\mathit{y}.[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{k}\,(\lambda\mathit{y}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{k}\,(\lambda\mathit{y}.[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\\
\textit{(induction on $t1$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

(app)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
(with
\mathit{f},\mathit{k}\,\#\,\mathit{t}
);

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}](\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}](\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k}))\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\lambda\mathit{k}.([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,\mathit{k})\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\lambda\mathit{k}.([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,\mathit{k})\\
\textit{(induction on $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$ and $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

\textit{(induction on $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$ and $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

∎

We now prove the operational correspondence as follows:

Proof.

We consider the theorem in an expanded form: for every
\mathit{t}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}}

then
\exists t^{\prime\prime}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\beta\textsc{g}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}\wedge{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}
.

The proof then follows by induction on reductions:

•

(beta)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\beta_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\;\equiv\;\;

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

\displaystyle\equiv\;\;

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{k^{\prime}}.\mathit{k^{\prime}}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}))\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

Then we construct the reduction sequence in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{}{(\lambda\mathit{k^{\prime}}.\mathit{k^{\prime}}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}))\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}}{\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{k^{\prime}}.\mathit{k^{\prime}}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}))\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congAbs}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{}{(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}(\lambda\mathit{f}.(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}}{\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{f}.(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congAbs}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,\mathit{k}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}}{\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{f}.(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{k}.([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,\mathit{k}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congAbs}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By
\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\eta}
and Lemma
B.5
then interpreting
the linear-base reduced term is equal to the graded-base
reduced term:
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\lambda\mathit{k}.([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,\mathit{k}
.

(congAppL)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

By induction on
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, we have

\exists\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\wedge\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
. Therefore, we construct
a chain of reductions in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{i}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{i}}}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}${}}}{\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{i}}}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congAbs}_{\textsf{G}}${}}\;\;\cdots\;\;\frac{\frac{\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{m}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}{\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{m}}}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}${}}}{\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{m}}}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congAbs}_{\textsf{G}}${}}\end{aligned}

where
m
is the length of the reduction sequence from
the induction.

satisfying the goal, since
\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

and therefore

\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\equiv\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})
.

(betaBox)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\Box\beta_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{k^{\prime}}.\mathit{k^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

Then we construct the reduction in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{}{(\lambda\mathit{k^{\prime}}.\mathit{k^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}}{\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{k^{\prime}}.\mathit{k^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congAbs}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}}{\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{k}.[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congAbs}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By
\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\eta}
and Lemma
B.5
then interpreting
the linear-base reduced term is equal to the graded-base
reduced term:
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\lambda\mathit{k}.[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})
.
