(congLetL)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congLet}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

By induction, analogously to the case for (congAppL).

∎

B.2.3.
Equation preservation

•

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\equiv_{\textsc{l}}\,[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\beta}$}{}}}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\{\textit{defn.}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{k}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}))\,(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

\displaystyle\{\text{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\beta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})

\displaystyle\{\text{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\beta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.((\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

\displaystyle\{\text{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\beta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,\mathit{k}

\displaystyle\{\text{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\eta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\{\textit{defn.}\}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

•

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\equiv_{\textsc{l}}\,[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\Box\beta}$}{}}}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\{\textit{defn.}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

\displaystyle\{\textit{defn.}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{k}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

\displaystyle\{\text{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\beta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\{\text{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\beta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k}

\displaystyle\{\text{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\eta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\{\textit{defn.}\}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

•

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\equiv_{\textsc{l}}\,\mathit{t}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\Box\eta}$}{}}}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\{\textit{defn.}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k})

\displaystyle\{\textit{defn.}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{k}.\mathit{k}\,\mathit{x})\,\mathit{k})

\displaystyle\{\textit{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\beta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{k}\,\mathit{x})

\displaystyle\{\textit{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\eta}${}}\}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{k}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathit{k}

\displaystyle\{\textit{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\eta}${}}\}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

•

All congruence rules follow straightforwardly by induction.

B.3.
Proof of Soundness for Linear Base to Graded Modal Base

See
3.2.1

B.3.1.
Type preservation

Lemma B.6.

For all linear-base contexts
\Gamma
, if
\mathrm{graded}(\Gamma,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})
then

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Proof.

By induction on the structure of
\Gamma
.

•

(empty context)
For
\emptyset
,
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\emptyset{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
is the graded context
\emptyset
. We have

\emptyset\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}\emptyset
, thus the goal is trivially satisfied.

•

(linear context extension)
For
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}
, the predicate
\mathrm{graded}((\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}),{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})
by
ex falso quodlibet
, the goal holds trivially.

•

(graded context extension)
For
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}
, given
\mathrm{graded}((\Gamma,\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}),{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})
, unification implies
\mathit{r}\equiv 0
, given this and the definition of
\mathsf{graded}
, we have
\mathrm{graded}(\Gamma,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})
. Applying this with induction on
\Gamma
we get
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
from which we conclude with the following reasoning steps:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0}}\mathit{A}

\displaystyle\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0}}\mathit{A}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0}}\mathit{A}

\displaystyle\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0}}\mathit{A})

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma,\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{0}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma,\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{0}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

∎

Type preservation then follows:

Proof.

By induction on the Linear Base typing:

•

(var)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{var${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

Therefore we construct the goal typing:

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{var${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

•

(abs)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{B}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{abs${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise we have

{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}
.

Therefore we construct the goal typing:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={abs${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

•

(app)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}&\;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{app${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premises we have

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

and

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Therefore we construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\quad{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={app${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

which matches the goal type by:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\text{unit property}&\equiv&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}+{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\text{homomorphism}&\equiv&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\end{array}

•

(weak)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}&\;\mathrm{graded}(\Gamma^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0})\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{weak${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise we have

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Therefore we construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}0\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={weak${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

which satisfies the goal by Lemma
B.6
.

•

(der)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{B}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma,\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{der${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise, we have
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

which gives the goal.

•

(pr)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}&\;\mathrm{graded}(\Gamma)\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{pr${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise we have
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Therefore we can construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={pr${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

which satisfies the goal since
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
(homomorphism).

•

(let)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}&\;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{let${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on both premises we have

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

and

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Therefore we can construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\quad{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}+{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={let${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

•

(approx)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma,\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{B}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}&\;\mathit{r}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\sqsubseteq}\,\mathit{s}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma,\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{B}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{s}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{approx${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise we have

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{s}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Therefore we can construct:

\frac{\begin{array}[]{cc}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\;\mathit{r}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\sqsubseteq}\,\mathit{s}\end{array}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{s}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={let${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

∎

B.3.2.
Operational correspondence

Lemma B.7 (Interpretation preserves substitution).

For all Linear Base terms
\mathit{t},\mathit{t^{\prime}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Proof.

By induction on the receiving term
\mathit{t^{\prime}}
:

•

(var)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\mathit{y}
:

–

\mathit{x}\equiv\mathit{y}
then we refine the goal:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{x}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{x}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{x}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{x}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(reflexivity)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}

–

\mathit{x}\not\equiv\mathit{y}
then we refine the goal:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathit{y}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{y}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathit{y}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{y}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathit{y}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\mathit{y}\\
\textit{(reflexivity)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}

•

(abs)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
(with
\mathit{y}\,\#\,\mathit{t}
);

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}](\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\lambda\mathit{y}.[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\lambda\mathit{y}.[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\lambda\mathit{y}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\lambda\mathit{y}.[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\lambda\mathit{y}.[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\lambda\mathit{y}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\lambda\mathit{y}.[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(induction on $t1$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

(app)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
;

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}](\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\\
\textit{(induction on $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$ and $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

(pr)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}

(let)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
(with
\mathit{y}\,\#\,\mathit{t}
);

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}](\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\

\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{in}\,[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\

\textit{(induction on $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$ and $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

∎

We now prove the operational correspondence as follows:

Proof.

We consider the theorem in an expanded form: for every
\mathit{t}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}}

then
\exists t^{\prime\prime}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}\wedge{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}
.

The proof then follows by induction on reductions:

•

(beta)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\beta_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

Then we construct the reduction in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By Lemma
B.7
then interpreting
the linear-base reduced term is equal to the graded-base
reduced term:
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

•

(congAppL)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

By induction on
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, we have

\exists\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\wedge\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
. Therefore, we construct
a chain of reductions in Graded Base:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{i}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{i}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}${}}\;\;\cdots\;\;\frac{\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{m}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}{\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}\,\mathit{m}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congApp}_{\textsf{L}}${}}

where
m
is the length of the reduction sequence from
the induction.

satisfying the goal, since
\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

and therefore

\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

•

(betaBox)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\Box\beta_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

Then we construct the reduction in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By Lemma
B.7
then interpreting
the linear-base reduced term is equal to the graded-base
reduced term:
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

•

(congLetL)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congLet}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

By induction, analogously to the case for (congAppL).

∎

All the congruence rules are simply by induction.

B.4.
Proof of Soundness for Linearity-Preserving translation of Linear Base to Graded Modal Base

See
3.2.2

B.4.1.
Type preservation

Proof.

By induction on the Linear Base typing:

•

(var)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\;\textsc{{{var${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

Therefore we construct the goal typing since the multiplicative unit is

\langle 1,1\rangle
now:

\frac{}{\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle}}\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{Right={var${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

•

(abs)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{B}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{abs${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premise we have

{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}}
.

Therefore we construct the goal typing:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{Right={abs${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

•

(app)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}&\;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{app${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premises we have

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}

and

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}
.

Therefore we construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\quad{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{Right={app${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

which matches the goal type by:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}+{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\langle 1,1\rangle\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\\
\text{unit property}&\equiv&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}+{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\\
\text{homomorphism}&\equiv&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}^{\prime}\end{array}

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}_{\square}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}&\;\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{g}_{\square}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}_{\square}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{let${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

By induction on the premises we have

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Therefore we can construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\quad{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}+{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={let${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}

∎

B.5.2.
Operational correspondence

Lemma B.8 (Interpretation preserves substitution).

For all graded modal base terms
\mathit{t},\mathit{t^{\prime}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

(betaBox)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}\Box}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\Box\beta_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

Then we construct the reduction in Linear Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{L}}${}}

By Lemma
B.3
then interpreting
the linear-base reduced term is equal to the graded-base
reduced term:
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

(congLetL)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}\Box}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}\Box}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congLet}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

By induction, analogously to the case for (congAppL).

B.5.3.
Equation preservation

Proof.

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\equiv_{\textsc{g}_{\Box}}\,[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\Box\beta}$}{}}}

3.2.3
(preservation of operational
semantics), since this includes the
\Box\beta
case as a reduction.

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\equiv_{\textsc{g}_{\Box}}\,\mathit{t}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{g}-\Box\eta}$}{}}}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}(\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}){\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\
\textit{\lx@text@lbrace defn. translation\lx@text@rbrace}&=\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\\
\textit{\lx@text@lbrace$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\Box\eta}$\lx@text@rbrace}&\equiv_{L}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\end{array}

•

Type preservation

cases that are new here:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:\forall\beta.({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\beta)\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}\beta}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip
