0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}1}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={tyapp${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}\quad\quad\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={abs${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}+{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}):{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{Right={app${}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}

The remaining cases follow as before, following the structure of the derivations.
∎

B.6.2.
Operational correspondence

Some intermediate lemmas are needed.

{lemm}

[][Interpretation preserves substitution of derivations]

For all terms
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and derivations
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}

and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}
then
there exists
\Pi^{\prime}
such that
\Pi^{\prime}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}

and

[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, generalising the admissibility of substitution.
∎

{lemm}

[][Type variables in the interpretation are irrelevant]

Given
\Pi^{\prime}:\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}

then for
\Pi:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}

formed from promotion of
\Pi^{\prime}

such that
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}=\Lambda\beta.\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

then
\beta\not\in\mathsf{fv}({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})
.

Proof.

By case analysis on
\Pi^{\prime}
; there is no part of the translation that
introduces a free type variable.
∎

Operational correspondence then follows by the following proof:

Proof.

•

(betaBox)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\Box\beta_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

with typing:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}\quad\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{right={let${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}

such that
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}

and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}

in the premises of this derivation
and
with
\Pi^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}
.

The interpretation of the reducing term’s proof is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv((\Lambda\beta.\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})

Then we have the following reduction sequence in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{\quad}{((\Lambda\beta.\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\beta](\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}))\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\text{{}}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{\quad}{([{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\beta](\lambda\mathit{f}.\mathit{f}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}))\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\beta]((\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{\quad}{[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\beta]((\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\beta][{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}

thus
\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}=[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\beta][{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

By Lemma
B.6.2
, type variables are irrelevant in the translation
and thus
[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\beta][{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}=[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

The goal is now that
\Pi^{\prime}=[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

which holds by Lemma
B.6.2
.

(congLetL)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congLet}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

with typing:

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}&\;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{let${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

such that
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}

and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{A}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]_{\mathit{r}}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}

and

\Pi^{\prime}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}
.

The interpretation of the reducing term and its target’s proofs are:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\equiv({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\equiv({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})

since by type preservation with
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
then

\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}
.

By induction on
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}
with
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}

and
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\square_{\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A}
, we then have

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Therefore, we construct
the reduction in Graded Base:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}({\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\text{@}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})\,(\lambda\mathit{x}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis})}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{congLet}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

satisfying the goal.

∎

B.6.3.
Equational correspondence

Note,
\eta
-equality for
\Box
is not preserved as described in the theorem statement.

Proof.

•

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\equiv_{\textsc{l}}\,[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\equiv_{\textsc{l}-\Box\beta}$}{}}}

Follows trivially from preservation of the operational semantics (Theorem
4.1.1
) since reduction is a subset of the equational theory.

Proof of Soundness for Graded Modal Core to Linear Push Core

5.6

B.7.1.
Type preservation

Proof.

The proof subsumes the earlier proofs; we add the additional cases here due to the
extension of the language from Graded Modal Base to Graded Modal Core.

(prod
i
)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}&\;\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle:\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{$\times$-i${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}&\;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle:\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{$\otimes$-i${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on both premises we have

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

and

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Therefore we can construct:

\frac{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\quad{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}{{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}+{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rangle:{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{A}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\otimes{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{B}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{Right={$\otimes$-i${}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}&\;\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{A},\mathit{y}:_{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}}}\mathit{B}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{C}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}\mathit{r}\cdot}\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{C}}\;\textsc{{{$\times$-e${}_{\textsf{G}}$}}{}}}

By induction on both premises we have

B.7.2.
Operational correspondence

Lemma B.9 (Interpretation preserves substitution).

For all Graded Base terms
\mathit{t},\mathit{t^{\prime}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

Proof.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\

&&\quad\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathsf{case}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{z}\rightarrow\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\\

&&\quad\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]\mathsf{case}\,\textsf{push}_{\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{y^{\prime}}\rightarrow\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{y^{\prime}}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{z^{\prime}}\rightarrow\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{z}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=\mathit{z^{\prime}}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\}\\

\textit{(ind. on $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$, $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$, $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

∎

The operational correspondence then follows via the following proof:

Proof.

The proof subsumes the earlier proofs; we add the additional cases here due to the
extension of the language from Graded Modal Base to Graded Modal Core.

For convenience, we expand the statement to
\mathit{t}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t^{\prime}}

then
\exists t^{\prime\prime}.{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}

\,\wedge\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}
.

•

(prodCong)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congProd}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

By induction, analogously to the case for (congAppL).

•

(prodBeta)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}][\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\beta\textsc{Prod}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

where
\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\,\#\,\mathit{t}
.
Then we construct the reduction in Linear Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}][{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{y}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta_{\textsf{L}}${}}

By Lemma
B.3
then interpreting
the linear-base reduced term is equal to the graded-base reduced term:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}][\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}][{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{y}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

(unitCong)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congUnit}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

By induction, analogously to the case for (congAppL).

(unitBeta)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\beta\textsc{Unit}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}

Then we construct the following reduction sequence (two reductions) in Linear Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta\textsc{Push}_{\mathsf{unit}\textsf{L}}${}}\quad\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{L}}${}}

By reflexivity then interpreting the linear-base reduced term is equal to the
graded-base reduced term.

(caseInj1)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{case}\,\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,\mathit{t}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{caseInj1}_{\textsf{G}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathsf{case}\,\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,\mathit{t}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv\mathsf{case}\,\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\}

Then we construct the reduction in Linear Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{case}\,\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{caseInj1}_{\textsf{L}}${}}

By Lemma
B.3
then interpreting
the linear-base reduced term is equal to the graded-base
reduced term:
{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\llbracket}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.4922,0.3047,0.6016}\rrbracket}
.

B.8.
Proof of Soundness for Linear Core to Graded Modal Core

See
5.7

B.8.1.
Type preservation

Proof.

The proof subsumes the earlier proofs; we add the additional cases here due to the
extension of the language from Linear Base to Linear Core.

•

(prod
i
)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{cc}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}&\;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}\end{array}\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\textsc{l}}\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle:\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\;\textsc{{{$\otimes$-i${}_{\textsf{L}}$}}{}}}

By induction on both premises we have

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{A}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

and

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\vdash_{\textsc{g}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}:{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{B}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Therefore we can construct:

B.8.2.
Operational correspondence

Lemma B.10 (Interpretation preserves substitution).

For all Linear Base terms
\mathit{t},\mathit{t^{\prime}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}
.

Proof.

We extend the proof of Lemma
B.7
with the new cases:

•

(
\otimes_{i}
)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle
;

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\langle[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\langle[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle\\

\textit{(induction on $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

(
\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}
)
\mathit{t^{\prime}}\equiv\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
(with
\mathit{y}\,\#\,\mathit{t}
);

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \begin{array}[]{rcrlll}\textit{(goal)}&&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. subst [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}([\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [lhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\\
\textit{(defn. interp [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle\\
\textit{(defn. subst [rhs])}&\Rightarrow&\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle&\hskip 2.0pt\equiv&\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}=[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{y}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle\\
\textit{(induction on $\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$)}&\Rightarrow&\top\end{array}\end{aligned}

∎

The operational correspondence then follows according to the following proof:

Proof.

The proof subsumes the earlier proofs; we add the additional cases here due to the
extension of the language from Linear Base to Linear Core.

We consider the theorem in an expanded form: for every
\mathit{t}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}}

then
\exists t^{\prime\prime}.{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{g}}^{\ast}\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}\wedge{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t^{\prime}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathit{t^{\prime\prime}}
.

(prodCong)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\\
\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{t^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\textsc{congProd}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

By induction, analogously to the case for (congAppL).

(prodBeta)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}][\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\beta_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

where
\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\,\#\,\mathit{t}
.
Then we construct the reduction in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}][{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{y}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta\textsc{Prod}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By Lemma
B.7
then interpreting
the graded-base reduced term is equal to the linear-base reduced term:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}][\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

\displaystyle\equiv[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}][{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{y}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

(unitBeta)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\;\textsc{{$\beta\textsc{Unit}_{\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}

Then we construct the reduction in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By reflexivity then interpreting the graded-base reduced term is equal to the
linear-base reduced term.

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{case}\,\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}/\mathit{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{caseInj1}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

then interpreting

(pushProd)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\textsf{push}_{\otimes}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rangle}\;\textsc{{$\beta\textsc{Push}_{\otimes\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is (with
\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathit{z}\,\#\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}&\;\mathit{z}\,\#\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\end{array}
):

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\textsf{push}_{\otimes}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{z}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rangle

Then we construct the following reduction sequence in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{z}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rangle\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle/\mathit{z}]\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rangle}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\mathit{x},\mathit{y}\rangle={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis},{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rangle\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rangle}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta\textsc{Prod}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By the definition of interpretation,
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\langle\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rangle\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\langle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]},{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rangle
.

(pushUnit)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\textsf{push}_{\mathrm{unit}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\langle\rangle}\;\textsc{{$\beta\textsc{Push}_{\mathsf{unit}\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is:

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\textsf{push}_{\otimes}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{x}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle

Then we construct the following reduction sequence in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\langle\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{x}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}[\langle\rangle/\mathit{x}]\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\mathit{x}\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}

\displaystyle\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,\langle\rangle=\langle\rangle\,\mathsf{in}\,\langle\rangle\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\langle\rangle}\quad\textsc{right=$\beta\textsc{Unit}_{\textsf{G}}${}}

By the definition of interpretation,
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\langle\rangle\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\langle\rangle
.

(pushSumInj1)

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\textsf{push}_{\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}}\;\textsc{{$\beta\textsc{push}_{\oplus 1\textsf{L}}$}{}}}

The interpretation of the reducing term is (
\mathit{z}\,\#\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
):

{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\textsf{push}_{\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{z}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{case}\,\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\}

Then we construct the following reduction sequence in Graded Base:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{}{\mathsf{let}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{z}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}={\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{case}\,\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{case}\,\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\}}\quad\textsc{right=$\Box\beta_{\textsf{G}}${}}\\
&\frac{}{\mathsf{case}\,\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\,\mathsf{of}\,\{\mathsf{inj1}\,\mathit{x}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{x}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]};\,\mathsf{inj2}\,\mathit{y}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{y}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\}\rightsquigarrow_{\textsc{l}}\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{caseInj1}_{\textsf{G}}${}}\end{aligned}

By the definition of interpretation,
{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}\equiv\mathsf{inj}_{1}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}[}{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\llparenthesis}\hskip 0.7pt\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\hskip 0.7pt{\color[rgb]{0.2188,0.4453,0.1016}\rrparenthesis}{\color[rgb]{0.0273,0.3125,0.8164}]}
.
